the toolsjar property may be used as a system-scoped dependency somewhere in the build plugins\n\n## Solution Approach\n\nThe project needs to be made compatible with Java 11+ by:\n\n1. **Option A (Recommended)**: Remove the tools.jar dependency entirely since it's not needed for Java 11+\n   - Update the parent pom.xml to remove the problematic profiles or make them conditional\n   - Disable system classpath references to tools.jar\n\n2. **Option B (Alternative)**: Update the profiles to be compatible with both Java 7 and Java 11+\n   - Add a profile for Java 11+ that doesn't depend on tools.jar\n   - Keep Java 7 compatibility if needed\n\n## Files Affected\n- /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml (parent POM with profiles and toolsjar property)\n\n## Impact\nThis is a configuration issue, not a code issue. The fix involves modifying build configuration only, not source code.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":896,"cache_read_input_tokens":59911,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":896,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3fb18ed0-f6ab-4d7c-a0f0-a4dfbd8fb6bc","uuid":"ad195490-a51e-48fc-944f-fe14fa2075b4"}

the toolsjar property may be used as a system-scoped dependency somewhere in the build plugins\n\n## Solution Approach\n\nThe project needs to be made compatible with Java 11+ by:\n\n1. **Option A (Recommended)**: Remove the tools.jar dependency entirely since it's not needed for Java 11+\n   - Update the parent pom.xml to remove the problematic profiles or make them conditional\n   - Disable system classpath references to tools.jar\n\n2. **Option B (Alternative)**: Update the profiles to be compatible with both Java 7 and Java 11+\n   - Add a profile for Java 11+ that doesn't depend on tools.jar\n   - Keep Java 7 compatibility if needed\n\n## Files Affected\n- /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml (parent POM with profiles and toolsjar property)\n\n## Impact\nThis is a configuration issue, not a code issue. the toolsjar property may be used as a system-scoped dependency somewhere in the build plugins\n\n## Solution Approach\n\nThe project needs to be made compatible with Java 11+ by:\n\n1. **Option A (Recommended)**: Remove the tools.jar dependency entirely since it's not needed for Java 11+\n   - Update the parent pom.xml to remove the problematic profiles or make them conditional\n   - Disable system classpath references to tools.jar\n\n2. **Option B (Alternative)**: Update the profiles to be compatible with both Java 7 and Java 11+\n   - Add a profile for Java 11+ that doesn't depend on tools.jar\n   - Keep Java 7 compatibility if needed\n\n## Files Affected\n- /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml (parent POM with profiles and toolsjar property)\n\n## Impact\nThis is a configuration issue, not a code issue. class Generated in package javax.annotation\n- **Root Cause**:\n  - The javax.annotation.Generated annotation was part of tools.jar in Java 7-10\n  - In Java 11+, this annotation is not available by default\n  - the toolsjar property may be used as a system-scoped dependency somewhere in the build plugins\n\n## Solution Approach\n\nThe project needs to be made compatible with Java 11+ by:\n\n1. **Option A (Recommended)**: Remove the tools.jar dependency entirely since it's not needed for Java 11+\n   - Update the parent pom.xml to remove the problematic profiles or make them conditional\n   - Disable system classpath references to tools.jar\n\n2. **Option B (Alternative)**: Update the profiles to be compatible with both Java 7 and Java 11+\n   - Add a profile for Java 11+ that doesn't depend on tools.jar\n   - Keep Java 7 compatibility if needed\n\n## Files Affected\n- /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml (parent POM with profiles and toolsjar property)\n\n## Impact\nThis is a configuration issue, not a code issue.
